		</a></div> <h2 class="mdc-heading mdc-lineup-module__headline mdc-heading--level-4 mdc-heading--bold" id="mdc-lineup-module__headline__UID__812"><a aria-describedby="mdc-lineup-module__deck__UID__812" class="mdc-link mds-link mds-link--no-underline" href="/articles/949410/cannabis-faces-significant-esg-risks" itemprop="url" tabindex="0"><span itemprop="name">Cannabis Faces Significant ESG Risks</span></a></h2> <div class="mdc-lineup-module__metadata"><a class="mdc-link mds-link mds-link--no-underline" href="/authors/1855/jon-hale" tabindex="0">Jon Hale</a></div></header> <div class="mdc-lineup-module__deck" id="mdc-lineup-module__deck__UID__812" itemprop="description"> 

	Industry would be wise to adopt sustainable practices from the beginning.
